    78	    pub(super) fn maintain(&mut self, source: &str, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    79	        self.receipt_pending_groups.clear();
    80	        // Inserts ordinarily charge their actual aggregate work. A deferred
    81	        // predicate cannot select that work on a device, so use the same public
    82	        // worst-case reservation as update/delete before skipping it. Already
    83	        // prepaid writes must not pay twice.
    84	        if self.device && !self.maintain_prepaid && self.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|
    85	            m.source == source && m.private.is_none() && m.row_key.is_none() && (self.schema.receipt_dependencies.contains_key(&m.target) || self.schema.tables[&m.target].sync.is_none())) {
    86	            self.reserve_maintains(source, &self.schema.tables[source])?;
    87	        }
    88	        self.maintain_row_views(source, old, new)?;
    89	        let maintains = self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|maintain| maintain.source == source && maintain.row_key.is_none());
    90	        for maintain in maintains {
    91	            if self.defer_receipt_maintain(maintain) || (self.device && self.schema.tables[&maintain.target].sync.is_none()) { continue; }
    92	            let source_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]);
    93	            let target = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.target];
    94	            let bytes = source_bytes.saturating_mul(2).saturating_add(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64));
    95	            let prepaid = self.maintain_prepaid;
    96	            if maintain.private.is_some() && self.column_prepaid { self.maintain_prepaid = true; }
    97	            let result = self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| runner.maintain_one(maintain, old, new));
    98	            self.maintain_prepaid = prepaid;
    99	            result?;
   100	        }
   101	        Ok(())
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — reserve interpreted images, both keys, targets and index buffers before allocation; release transient ownership on every exit.
   105	    pub(super) fn maintain_scratch<T>(&mut self, bytes: u64, work: impl FnOnce(&mut Self) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   106	        if self.maintain_prepaid { return work(self); }
   107	        self.meter.heap(bytes)?;
   108	        let result = work(self);
   109	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = self.meter.used.heap_bytes.saturating_sub(bytes);
   110	        result
   111	    }
   112	
   113	    fn maintain_one(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, old: Option<&Value>, new: Option<&Value>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   114	        let table = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   115	        let mut changes: Vec<(Vec<Value>, i128)> = Vec::new();
   116	        for (image, direction) in [(old, -1), (new, 1)] {
   117	            let Some(image) = image else { continue };
   118	            self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   119	            let image = crate::schema::row_image(image, table);
   120	            if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   121	            let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   122	            let change = delta(maintain, &image, direction)?;
   123	            if let Some(existing) = changes.iter_mut().find(|(k, _)| *k == key) { existing.1 += change; }
   124	            else { changes.push((key, change)); }
   125	        }
   126	        for (key, change) in changes {
   127	            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   128	            if let Some(retiring) = self.retiring.as_mut() {
   129	                let entry = retiring.aggregates.entry((maintain.target.clone(), canonical_json(&key))).or_insert_with(|| (key, 0));
   130	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   131	                continue;
   132	            }
   133	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   134	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   135	            }
   136	        }
   137	        Ok(())
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    /// Apply a retirement's net aggregate changes, one per target row, in
   141	    /// key order. Only a total the commit really ends with can overflow.
   142	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   143	    pub(super) fn apply_retired_aggregates(&mut self, changes: BTreeMap<(String, String), (Vec<Value>, i128)>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   144	        let schema = self.schema;
   145	        for ((target, _), (key, change)) in changes {
   146	            let maintain = schema.maintain_for(&target).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing maintain for {target}")))?;
   147	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   148	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, None)?;
   149	            }
   150	        }
   151	        Ok(())
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   155	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain_reported(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, report: &mut crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   156	        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   157	        self.rebuilt_source_rows = 0;
   158	        self.rebuild_maintain(maintain)?;
   159	        let rows = self.rebuilt_source_rows;
   160	        report.views.push(crate::store::adoption_report::ViewBuild { name: maintain.label(), rows, elapsed: started.elapsed() });
   161	        Ok(())
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    /// Build one aggregate from the rows in the store: the target's rows go,
   165	    /// then every source row contributes once. A schema change that adds or
   166	    /// redefines a `maintain` calls this, so a count over existing rows is
   167	    /// right from its first read (day two, LLP 2000.000 §11.2).
   168	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   169	    pub(crate) fn rebuild_maintain(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   170	        let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   171	        let saved = self.column_rebuild;
   172	        self.column_rebuild |= maintain.private.is_some();
   173	        let result = self.rebuild_maintain_inner(maintain);
   174	        self.column_rebuild = saved;
   175	        self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   176	        result
   177	    }
   178	
   179	    #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
   180	    fn rebuild_maintain_inner(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   181	        if maintain.row_key.is_some() { return self.rebuild_row_view(maintain); }
   182	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");
   183	        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
   184	        let target_bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(target).saturating_mul(16 + 4 * target.indexes.len() as u64);
   185	        self.meter.heap(target_bytes.saturating_mul(256))?;
   186	        loop {
   187	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.target, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   188	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   189	            for (existing, key) in page {
   190	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   191	                self.refuse_timeline_write(&maintain.target, crate::schema::Effect::Delete)?;
   192	                let entries = self.tx.delete(&maintain.target, target, &existing)?;
   193	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::IndexEntries, entries)?;
   194	                self.row_changed(&maintain.target, Some(&existing), None, Origin::Maintained)?;
   195	            }
   196	        }
   197	        let source = self.schema.table(&maintain.source).expect("validated");
   198	        self.meter.heap(super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(source).saturating_mul(256))?;
   199	        let mut totals: BTreeMap<String, (Vec<Value>, i128)> = BTreeMap::new();
   200	        bounds = Bounds::default();
   201	        loop {
   202	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &maintain.source, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
   203	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
   204	            for (mut image, key) in page {
   205	                self.rebuilt_source_rows += 1;
   206	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   207	                materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, source);
   208	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   209	                self.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Steps, maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
   210	                if !maintain.selected(&image) { continue; }
   211	                // @ref LLP 3000.028#6 — a total unique owner key has one contribution; backfill streams bounded pages instead of retaining all lifetime keys in a totals map.
   212	                if maintain.own_keys.is_some() {
   213	                    let heap = self.meter.used.heap_bytes;
   214	                    self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   215	                    self.apply_aggregate(maintain, maintain.source_key(&image), 1, Some(true))?;
   216	                    self.meter.used.heap_bytes = heap;
   217	                    continue;
   218	                }
   219	                self.meter.heap(target_bytes)?;
   220	                let key = maintain.source_key(&image);
   221	                let change = delta(maintain, &image, 1)?;
   222	                let entry = match totals.entry(canonical_json(&key)) {
   223	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Occupied(entry) => {
   224	                        self.meter.used.heap_bytes -= target_bytes;
   225	                        entry.into_mut()
   226	                    }
   227	                    std::collections::btree_map::Entry::Vacant(entry) => entry.insert((key, 0)),
   228	                };
   229	                entry.1 = entry.1.checked_add(change).ok_or_else(|| aggregate_overflow(maintain))?;
   230	            }
   231	        }
   232	        for (_, (key, change)) in totals {
   233	            if change != 0 || self.live_sum(maintain) {
   234	                self.apply_aggregate(maintain, key, change, Some(true))?;
   235	            }
   236	        }
   237	        Ok(())
   238	    }
   239	
   240	    fn live_sum(&self, maintain: &Maintain) -> bool {
   241	        matches!(maintain.kind, MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && self.schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring())
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    /// A zero sum is still a group while source rows remain: one signed
   245	    /// contribution may expire before the other. Keep its candidate row so
   246	    /// a scan, snapshot and offline replica can discover the later live sum.
   247	    fn aggregate_source_present(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   248	        // One returned source image and its encoded key (MemoryRows also materializes count_prefix).
   249	        let bytes = super::write_target::row_copy_ceiling(&self.schema.tables[&maintain.source]).saturating_mul(2);
   250	        self.maintain_scratch(bytes, |runner| {
   251	            let source = &runner.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   252	            let index = maintain.contribution_index();
   253	            if !source.indexes.contains_key(&index) { return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!("maintain {} needs its contribution index", maintain.target))); }
   254	            runner.charge_maintain(MeterKind::Examined, 1)?;
   255	            let present = runner.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.count_prefix(allowance, &maintain.source, &index, key, 1))? != 0;
   256	            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9 — a local positive witness keeps zero; absence needs complete, currently held source coverage.
   257	            if !present && runner.device && !runner.aggregate_source_complete(maintain, key)? {
   258	                runner.unknown_fact(&maintain.source, &index, key, "zero-sum occupancy");
   259	                return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("the zero-sum group's source occupancy is not known locally; queued without a predicted row".into()));
   260	            }
   261	            Ok(present)
   262	        })
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    fn aggregate_source_complete(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: &[Value]) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
   266	        let source = &self.schema.tables[&maintain.source];
   267	        if source.sync.as_ref().is_none_or(|sync| sync.horizon.is_some()) || self.schema.maintain_subset_unknown(&maintain.target) { return Ok(false); }
   268	        // The target has the same bare audience keys, but no source horizon.
   269	        if !self.upsert_absence_proven(&maintain.target, "byGroup", &target_group_key(maintain, key))? { return Ok(false); }
   270	        for (name, index) in &source.indexes {
   271	            let values = index.columns().iter().map_while(|column| maintain.by.iter().position(|field| field == column).map(|n| key[n].clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   272	            if self.retained_group(&maintain.source, name, &values)? { return Ok(false); }
   273	        }
   274	        Ok(true)
   275	    }
   276	
   277	    fn apply_aggregate(&mut self, maintain: &Maintain, key: Vec<Value>, change: i128, source_present: Option<bool>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   278	        if self.schema.timelines.iter().any(|t| t.owns(&maintain.target)) {
   279	            return Err(super::timeline::refusal("E_TIMELINE_OWNED", "timeline-owned tables cannot be aggregate targets; maintain a mapped application table instead"));
   280	        }
   281	        let id = maintained_id(&key);
   282	        let target = self.schema.table(&maintain.target).expect("validated");

   324	pub(super) fn delta(maintain: &Maintain, source: &Value, direction: i64) -> Result<i128, RuntimeError> {

     1	//! Internal contribution format adoption under the evaluation adapter's writer lock.
     2	use super::store::Tx;
     3	use crate::{error::{MeterKind, RuntimeError}, meter::{Ceilings, Cut, Meter}, rows::{adoption, word_heap as heap}, schema::{IndexComponent, Schema, Table}, value::{Value, canonical_json}};
     4	
     5	pub(super) fn install(tx: &Tx<'_>, schema: &mut Schema) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
     6	    let backend = tx.query("SELECT schema,generation FROM _backend WHERE singleton", &[])?;
     7	    let previous: Schema = super::store::decode(backend[0].get(0))?;
     8	    let mut generation = backend[0].get::<_, i64>(1);
     9	    schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
    10	    if *schema != previous {
    11	        for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
    12	            for (index_name, index) in &table.indexes {
    13	                let Some(IndexComponent::Contribution(maintain)) = index.components.first() else {continue};
    14	                if previous.table(name).and_then(|t|t.index(index_name)) == Some(index) {continue;}
    15	                tx.execute("DELETE FROM i WHERE t = $1 AND ix = $2", &[name,index_name])?;
    16	                let work=adoption::Work::DECODE.with_contribution(maintain);
    17	                let mut after: Option<String> = None;
    18	                loop {
    19	                    let mut meter=Meter::new(Ceilings::job(),Cut::Abort);
    20	                    meter.at(&format!("adopt {name}.{index_name}"));
    21	                    meter.heap(4096 + after.as_deref().map_or(0, heap::text))?;
    22	                    let page=source_page(&tx,name,table,after.as_deref(),&mut meter,work)?;
    23	                    if page.is_empty() { break; }
    24	                    for raw in &page {
    25	                        let scratch=meter.mark();
    26	                        let id=raw.get::<_,&str>(0);
    27	                        let data=raw.get::<_,&str>(1);
    28	                        meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined,1)?;
    29	                        adoption::charge(&mut meter, adoption::decode_bytes(data))?;
    30	                        let row: Value=super::store::decode(data)?;
    31	                        meter.charge(MeterKind::Examined,1)?;
    32	                        meter.step(maintain.evaluation_steps())?;
    33	                        adoption::charge(&mut meter, adoption::contribution_bytes(&row,table))?;
    34	                        for key in crate::rows::stored_index_keys(table,index,&row,id) {
    35	                            if key.len() > crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES { return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(name,index_name,1)); }
    36	                            meter.charge(MeterKind::IndexEntries,1)?;
    37	                            tx.execute("INSERT INTO i(t,ix,k,id) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4)", &[name,index_name,&key,&id])?;
    38	                        }
    39	                        drop(row);
    40	                        meter.release_heap(scratch);
    41	                    }
    42	                    let last=page.last().expect("nonempty page").get::<_,&str>(0);
    43	                    meter.heap(heap::text(last))?;
    44	                    after=Some(last.to_owned());
    45	                    // The full driver-owned page remains reserved until every
    46	                    // decoded row and its index scratch have been consumed.
    47	                    drop(page);
    48	                }
    49	            }
    50	        }
    51	        generation=generation.checked_add(1).ok_or_else(||RuntimeError::Generation("generation overflow".into()))?;
    52	        tx.execute("UPDATE _backend SET schema = $1, generation = $2 WHERE singleton", &[&canonical_json(schema), &generation])?;
    53	    }
    54	    u64::try_from(generation).map_err(|_|RuntimeError::Generation("negative generation".into()))
    55	}
    56	
    57	// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.9.3 — adoption reserves fetched images separately from per-row contribution scratch.
    58	pub(super) fn source_page(tx: &super::store::Tx<'_>, table: &str, schema: &Table, after: Option<&str>, meter: &mut Meter, work: adoption::Work) -> Result<Vec<postgres::Row>, RuntimeError> {
    59	    let sizes=if after.is_some() {
    60	        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id) + octet_length(data)),0)::bigint, COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=$1 AND id>$2 ORDER BY id LIMIT $3) AS picked"
    61	    } else {
    62	        "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id) + octet_length(data)),0)::bigint, COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=$1 AND $2::text IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT $3) AS picked"
    63	    };
    64	    let mut limit=32i64;
    65	    let page_mark=meter.mark();
    66	    loop {
    67	        limit=work.limit(meter,limit as usize) as i64;
    68	        let size=tx.query(sizes,&[&table,&after,&limit])?;
    69	        let bytes=size[0].get::<_,i64>(0) as u64;
    70	        let count=size[0].get::<_,i64>(1) as u64;
    71	        // Network/read buffers, retained driver rows and their metadata. Typed
    72	        // JSON decode is priced from borrowed text later, one row at a time;
    73	        // the query scan's conservative 256x all-rows decoder is inappropriate
    74	        // for this walk. This initial estimate is followed by a scratch check
    75	        // before any adoption callback runs.
    76	        let reservation=bytes.saturating_mul(8).saturating_add(count.saturating_mul(1024));
    77	        if limit>1 && reservation>meter.ceilings.heap_bytes/2 { limit/=2; continue; }
    78	        meter.heap(reservation)?;
    79	        let query=if after.is_some() {
    80	            "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=$1 AND id>$2 ORDER BY id LIMIT $3"
    81	        } else {
    82	            "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=$1 AND $2::text IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT $3"
    83	        };
    84	        let page=tx.query(query,&[&table,&after,&limit])?;
    85	        if adoption::page_fits(page.iter().map(|raw| raw.get::<_,&str>(1)), meter, work,
    86	            Some(&|row| adoption::contribution_bytes(row,schema)))? { return Ok(page); }
    87	        limit=(page.len() as i64/2).max(1);
    88	        drop(page);
    89	        meter.release_heap(page_mark.clone());
    90	    }
    91	}

   230	    pub(crate) fn authorized_scan<T: Default>(&mut self, allowance: &[(MeterKind, u64)], scan: impl FnOnce(&mut Self, &Authorization) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>,
   231	    ) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   232	        self.prepaid_scan(allowance, true, scan)
   233	    }
   234	
   235	    /// Public or already-authorized data reads still reserve their logical
   236	    /// meter; they do no authorization traversal. A nested scope cannot opt
   237	    /// out of its enclosing authorization's physical allowance.
   238	    pub(super) fn prepaid_scan<T: Default>(&mut self, allowance: &[(MeterKind, u64)], physical: bool, scan: impl FnOnce(&mut Self, &Authorization) -> Result<T, RuntimeError>,
   239	    ) -> Result<T, RuntimeError> {
   240	        // This meter is the device's separate physical-work budget. Its
   241	        // caller maps exhaustion to uncertainty, never a logical E_BOUND.
   242	        if self.device_work_active {
   243	            let active = self.tx.authorizing.replace(self.tx.authorizing.get() || (physical && !self.context.is_system()));
   244	            let result = scan(self, &Authorization::internal());
   245	            self.tx.authorizing.set(active);
   246	            return result;
   247	        }
   248	        for &(kind, amount) in allowance {
   249	            if !self.read_charge(kind, amount)? { return Ok(T::default()); }
   250	        }
   251	        if self.read_capped() { return Ok(T::default()); }
   252	        let scope = Authorization { probes: Cell::new(0), remaining_probes: self.meter.ceilings.rule_probes.saturating_sub(self.meter.used.rule_probes) };
   253	        let state = self.enter_prepaid(physical);
   254	        let result = scan(self, &scope);
   255	        self.leave_prepaid(state, result.as_ref().err());
   256	        if scope.probes.get() > 0 { self.read_charge(MeterKind::RuleProbes, scope.probes.get())?; }
   257	        result
   258	    }
   259	
   260	    // Kept out of the generic closure wrapper: every protected access shares
   261	    // the same scope transition instead of instantiating its restoration code.
   262	    #[inline(never)]
   263	    pub(super) fn enter_prepaid(&mut self, physical: bool) -> PrepaidState {
   264	        let nested = self.caller_heap_ceiling.is_some();
   265	        self.caller_heap_ceiling.get_or_insert((self.meter.ceilings.heap_bytes, self.meter.used.heap_bytes));
   266	        PrepaidState {
   267	            mark: self.meter.mark(),
   268	            ceilings: std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter.ceilings, Ceilings::unbounded()),
   269	            cut: std::mem::replace(&mut self.meter.cut, Cut::Abort),
   270	            authorizing: self.tx.authorizing.replace(self.tx.authorizing.get() || (physical && !self.context.is_system())),
   271	            nested,
   272	        }
   273	    }
   274	
   275	    #[inline(never)]
   276	    pub(super) fn leave_prepaid(&mut self, state: PrepaidState, error: Option<&RuntimeError>) {
   277	        self.tx.authorizing.set(state.authorizing);
   278	        self.meter.restore(state.mark);
   279	        self.meter.ceilings = state.ceilings;
   280	        self.meter.cut = state.cut;
   281	        if !state.nested { self.caller_heap_ceiling = None; }
   282	        if let Some(RuntimeError::Bound { kind: MeterKind::HeapBytes, site, .. }) = error {
   283	            self.meter.capped_at.get_or_insert((MeterKind::HeapBytes, site.clone()));
   284	        }
   285	    }
   286	
   287	}

     2	// @ref LLP 3000.014#7-activation — publish captures, indexes and generation in one transaction.
     3	use super::*;
     4	
     5	impl PostgresEngine {
     6	    pub fn read_through_shortened(&mut self) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, u64>, RuntimeError> {
     7	        let tx = self.store.transaction(None)?;
     8	        let counts = decode(
     9	            tx.query(
    10	                "SELECT through_shortened FROM _backend WHERE singleton",
    11	                &[],
    12	            )?[0]
    13	                .get(0),
    14	        )?;

    18	    pub fn sweep_expired(&mut self, now: i64, limit: usize) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    19	        let tx = self.store.transaction(Some(WriteMode::Serialized))?;
    20	        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
    21	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
    22	            &tx,
    23	            &self.schema,
    24	            Context::system().at(now),
    25	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
    26	            seq,
    27	        );
    28	        let mut expiry_after = self.expiry_after.clone();
    29	        let count = runner.sweep_server_rows(limit, &mut expiry_after)?;
    30	        let events = runner.events.take();
    31	        let tables: Vec<_> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
    32	        let writes = runner.rows_written;
    33	        drop(runner);
    34	        if writes > 0 {
    35	            tx.record_commit(
    36	                seq,
    37	                now,
    38	                "expire",
    39	                crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL,
    40	                "sweep",
    41	                &tables,
    42	                writes,
    43	            )?;
    44	            partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
    45	        }
    46	        tx.commit()?;
    47	        self.expiry_after = expiry_after;
    48	        Ok(count)
    49	    }

    70	            let table = self

     3	// @ref LLP 3000.014#7-activation — stage the entire generation and publish only on success.
     4	use super::*;
     5	use snapback4_core::{
     6	    engine::{Context, runner::Runner, through_activation},

     9	
    10	impl MemoryStore {
    11	    pub fn seed_server_at(
    12	        &mut self,
    13	        rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
    14	        now: i64,
    15	    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    16	        let schema = self
    17	            .schema
    18	            .clone()
    19	            .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema("install a server schema before seeding".into()))?;
    20	        let tx = self.transaction()?;
    21	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
    22	            &tx,
    23	            &schema,
    24	            Context::system().at(now),
    25	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
    26	            0,
    27	        );
    28	        let count = runner.seed_server_rows(rows)?;
    29	        drop(runner);
    30	        tx.commit()?;
    31	        Ok(count)
    32	    }
    33	    pub fn sweep_server_at(&mut self, now: i64, limit: usize) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    34	        let schema = self.schema.clone().ok_or_else(|| {
    35	            RuntimeError::Schema("install a server schema before sweeping".into())
    36	        })?;
    37	        let mut expiry_after = self.expiry_after.clone();
    38	        let tx = self.transaction()?;
    39	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
    40	            &tx,
    41	            &schema,
    42	            Context::system().at(now),
    43	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
    44	            0,
    45	        );
    46	        let count = runner.sweep_server_rows(limit, &mut expiry_after)?;
    47	        drop(runner);
    48	        tx.commit()?;
    49	        self.expiry_after = expiry_after;
    50	        Ok(count)
    51	    }
    52	    pub fn activate_server_at(
    53	        &mut self,
    54	        mut schema: Schema,
    55	        now: i64,
    56	    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    57	        schema.prepare_contribution_indexes();
    58	        if let Some(issue) = snapback4_core::schema::validate(&schema).first() {
    59	            return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(format!(
    60	                "{}: {}",
    61	                issue.path, issue.message
    62	            )));
    63	        }
    64	        let previous = self.schema.clone();
    65	        let mut staged = Self {
    66	            state: RefCell::new(self.state.borrow().clone()),
    67	            schema: previous.clone(),
    68	            changes: RefCell::new(self.changes.borrow().clone()),
    69	            drained: Cell::new(self.drained.get()),
    70	            expiry_after: BTreeMap::new(),
    71	        };
    72	        let tx = staged.transaction()?;
    73	        let shortened = through_activation::activate(&tx, &schema, previous.as_ref(), now)?;
    74	        tx.commit()?;
    75	        staged.install(&schema, &BTreeMap::new(), false)?;
    76	        let tx = staged.transaction()?;
    77	        for (name, table) in &schema.tables {
    78	            let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
    79	            loop {
    80	                let page = tx.scan(name, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256)?;
    81	                if page.is_empty() {
    82	                    break;
    83	                }
    84	                for (old, key) in page {
    85	                    bounds.after = key::decode(&key);
    86	                    let mut row = old.clone();
    87	                    table.recompute_deadline(&mut row)?;
    88	                    snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(
    89	                        &schema,
    90	                        name,
    91	                        &snapback4_core::schema::row_image(&row, table),
    92	                    )?;
    93	                    tx.put(name, table, &row, Some(&old))?;
    94	                }
    95	            }
    96	        }
    97	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
    98	            &tx,
    99	            &schema,
   100	            Context::system().at(now),
   101	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
   102	            0,
   103	        );
   104	        runner.rebuild_server_derivations(previous.as_ref())?;
   105	        drop(runner);
   106	        through_activation::validate_indexes(&tx, &schema)?;
   107	        let generation = tx
   108	            .meta("server:generation")?
   109	            .and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok())
   110	            .unwrap_or(0)
   111	            .checked_add(1)
   112	            .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("generation overflow".into()))?;
   113	        tx.set_meta("server:generation", &generation.to_string())?;
   114	        tx.set_meta("read_through_shortened", &stored(&shortened))?;
   115	        tx.commit()?;
   116	        *self = staged;
   117	        Ok(generation)
   118	    }
   119	
   120	    /// Recompute effective instants, validate every immutable capture against
   121	    /// the final image, then atomically publish. This never resolves a capture.
   122	    pub fn restore_server_at(
   123	        &mut self,
   124	        rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = (String, Value)>,
   125	        now: i64,
   126	    ) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   127	        let schema = self.schema.clone().ok_or_else(|| {
   128	            RuntimeError::Schema("install a server schema before restoring".into())
   129	        })?;
   130	        let tx = self.transaction()?;
   131	        let mut count = 0;
   132	        for (name, mut row) in rows {
   133	            let table = schema
   134	                .table(&name)
   135	                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("missing {name}")))?;
   136	            table.recompute_deadline(&mut row)?;
   137	            snapback4_core::engine::validate::validate_row(&schema, &name, &row)?;
   138	            let old = tx.get(&name, row.field("id")?.as_str()?)?;
   139	            tx.put(&name, table, &row, old.as_ref())?;
   140	            count += 1;
   141	        }
   142	        through_activation::validate_restore(&tx, &schema, now)?;
   143	        let mut runner = Runner::new(
   144	            &tx,
   145	            &schema,
   146	            Context::system().at(now),
   147	            Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort),
   148	            0,
   149	        );
   150	        runner.rebuild_server_derivations(None)?;
   151	        drop(runner);
   152	        through_activation::validate_indexes(&tx, &schema)?;
   153	        tx.commit()?;
   154	        Ok(count)
   155	    }
   156	}

The formal same-ID retirement composes through a `Steps` chain. I found a separate expiry-model concern: ordinary Lean deletes still walk the unfiltered physical store, while runtime reference actions exclude expired children. I’m checking that path and the probe reservation accounting before finalizing findings.

   587	
   588	    /// Delete the rows past their `expire` column. They were absent to
   589	    /// every reader from the moment they expired; the sweep makes that
   590	    /// physical, so the log carries the deletion to each device holding
   591	    /// them. At most `limit` rows go in one commit; the next pass continues.
   592	    pub fn sweep_expired(&mut self, now: i64, limit: usize) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   593	        self.sweep_expired_for(now, limit, std::time::Duration::MAX)
   594	    }
   595	
   596	    /// Stop between row removals; a short sweep can still leave due rows.
   597	    pub fn sweep_expired_for(&mut self, now: i64, limit: usize, budget: std::time::Duration) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
   598	        // Expiry cleanup is recovery work. Pin the same authoritative clock,
   599	        // but commit physical cleanup before attempting a due opening so a
   600	        // refused publication can never wedge the rows whose upper lifetime
   601	        // has already ended.
   602	        let now = self.pin_time_audience(now)?;
   603	        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
   604	        let expiring: Vec<(String, String)> = self.schema.through_order().into_iter().rev().filter(|name| self.schema.tables[name].ephemeral.is_none()).filter_map(|name| {
   605	                self.schema.tables[&name].expiry_index().map(|(index, _)| (name.clone(), index.to_owned()))
   606	            }).collect();
   607	        if expiring.is_empty() {
   608	            return Ok(0);
   609	        }
   610	        let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
   611	        let now = time_audience::pin_writer_time(&self.schema, &tx, now)?;
   612	        let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
   613	        let mut runner = Runner::new(&tx, &self.schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
   614	        let mut removed = 0_u64;
   615	        let mut visited = 0_usize;
   616	        let mut expiry_after = self.expiry_after.clone();
   617	        let due = crate::store::Bounds { gte: Some(Value::Int(i64::MIN)), lte: Some(Value::Int(now)), ..Default::default() };
   618	        let expiring = through_activation::resume_expiry_tables(
   619	            expiring.into_iter().map(|(name, _)| name).collect(), &expiry_after,
   620	        ).into_iter().map(|name| {
   621	            let index = self.schema.tables[&name].expiry_index().expect("expiring table").0.to_owned();
   622	            (name, index)
   623	        }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   624	        'tables: for (table_name, index) in &expiring {
   625	            let mut bounds = due.clone();
   626	            bounds.after = expiry_after.get(table_name).cloned();
   627	            let mut wrapped = bounds.after.is_none();
   628	            loop {
   629	                let batch = (limit - visited).min(256);
   630	                if batch == 0 {
   631	                    break 'tables;
   632	                }
   633	                let rows = tx.scan(table_name, index, &[], &bounds, crate::store::Dir::Asc, batch)?;
   634	                let count = rows.len();
   635	                for (row, key) in rows {
   636	                    bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
   637	                    if let Some(after) = &bounds.after { expiry_after.insert(table_name.clone(), after.clone()); }
   638	                    visited += 1;
   639	                    through_activation::mark_expiry_table(&mut expiry_after, table_name);
   640	                    if removed > 0 && started.elapsed() >= budget { break 'tables; }
   641	                    match runner.retire_expired_row(table_name, &row) {
   642	                        Ok(true) => removed += 1,
   643	                        Ok(false) => {},
   644	                        // An expected reference/read-through blocker retains
   645	                        // the logically expired root. Continue by key so it
   646	                        // cannot wedge cleanup of unrelated due rows.
   647	                        Err(RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_REFERENCED" | "E_PARENT_HAS_CHILDREN", .. }) => {},
   648	                        Err(error) => return Err(error),
   649	                    }
   650	                }
   651	                if count < batch {
   652	                    expiry_after.remove(table_name);
   653	                    if !wrapped && visited < limit {
   654	                        bounds = due.clone();
   655	                        wrapped = true;
   656	                        continue;
   657	                    }
   658	                    break;
   659	                }
   660	            }
   661	        }
   662	        let events = runner.events.take();
   663	        let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
   664	        let written = runner.rows_written;
   665	        drop(runner);
   666	        if written > 0 {
   667	            tx.record_commit(seq, now, "expire", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "sweep", &tables, written)?;
   668	            partition::append(&tx, seq, &events)?;
   669	        }
   670	        tx.commit()?;
   671	        self.expiry_after = expiry_after;
   672	        self.advance_time_audience(now)?;
   673	        Ok(removed)
   674	    }

# Views: plan, maintain or materialize

**Start here:** Use `view` for derived data: filters and unions plan reads, while counts update in the source transaction. For public post totals, use this excerpt from the complete example below; do not maintain counters in client writes.

```quarry
view postCount: count posts by .author
query total(author: principal):
  return postCount[author]
```

- `E_MAINTAIN_READ_RULE`: grouping lost an authorization field; retain it as a bare key or declare the aggregate's intended read rule.
- `E_QUARRY_SYNTAX`: a view cannot have its own sync line; keep inherited sync or choose `online only` and lose offline coverage.
- `N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED`: the aggregate does not inherit the source horizon; measure it, or choose `online only` only if offline reads and prediction by the named dependent mutations are unnecessary (`predicts` then fails with `E_NOT_PREDICTABLE`).

## Reference

```quarry
table posts:
  author: principal
  body: text <=500
  at: time
  removed: bool default false
  public 'public posts'
  online only
table reposts:
  actor: principal
  post: posts
  at: time
  public 'public reposts'
  online only
view activity:
  posts where not .removed { post: .id, actor: .author, at, repost: false }
  reposts { post, actor, at, repost: true }
query global(c: cursor ?):
  return activity last 20 by .at after c
view postCount: count posts by .author
query total(author: principal):
  return postCount[author]
```

A filter or projection is planned when read. Branches form a union without a copied application table. An aggregate is maintained in the source write's transaction. Public Following is materialized per reader when its source and edge rules admit that disclosure (`guide feeds`).

Each union branch runs under its own source's read rule before filtering or projection. Branches have compatible fields and types. Identity includes both branch and source id; equal ids in different tables remain distinct events. The exposed `id` is the source id. Include a branch discriminator, such as `repost`, when forming a client list key. A predicate on a projected column reaches each source; a constant predicate selects branches. Order fields must map to source columns in every branch.

A maintained view has rows `{ id, <group columns>, value }`. A full group key returns a scalar. An authorized miss is zero; a hidden hit and hidden miss are both null. Omit `by` for one global scalar such as `view totalPosts: count posts`, read it by its bare name, and receive zero for authorized absence; its compiler-owned physical group key is not an application column. Pages return existing groups, using `first`/`last`, `by .column`
and `after c`. A domain may use an inherited product cap or the runtime meter.
Source caps can bound distinct groups; no new target cap is enforced.

During adoption, query and sync snapshot reads keep reading the previous backend. A newly added query answers pending until the views are complete. Writes wait, then execute once.
`check` reports N_ADOPTION_PAUSE; dev and deploy print it before activating. Plan the write pause before adopting. Source counts estimate work at measured eb1000 rates; the subtotal is not a floor. The modeled “up to” time is not an SLO or hard bound. Removed or rebuilt rows/indexes add an explicitly unestimated retirement pause, including tables whose names survive. Tables affected by read-through or inherited-expiry changes warn about retirement/recapture: the deletion subset depends on activation time, so their stored populations are not exact deletion counts. On a schema change, an unaffected read-through table separately rewrites every stored row to recompute deadlines; this rewrite count is exact, not a deletion subset. Both pauses remain unestimated. Maintained-column mirrors, cap resets and asset-home retirement also warn when unpriced. Payload width, fanout, cache and WAL/storage affect time.
Activation defaults to a 64 MiB combined main/temp cache target (half each). Set SNAPBACK4_ADOPTION_CACHE_MIB to 0–512; 0 keeps caller settings and larger caller caches are retained. Linux reduces the target for address-space headroom. Workspace is not an RSS cap: the prior 64 MiB main-cache run used about 147 MiB process RSS; sorter RAM follows the cache. SQLite NOMEM after enlargement rolls back and retries once at caller settings; choose 0 for constrained hosts.
The adoption line reports phases, capture fills and rebuilt views with source visits and milliseconds; nested details are included in their parent phase. A failed or interrupted attempt keeps the previous backend; restart retries the complete build.
Signed-in `GET /changes` waits for the whole adoption because reconnecting
requires a durable presence write. That wait grows with the store; it is not
bounded by a rebuild chunk. Query and sync reads have reserved admission capacity
so queued mutations cannot occupy all their request slots.

Omit `read` to derive readability: a public/allow source gives `read <- allow`;
otherwise every field in its read rule must survive as a bare group key, and
that same rule protects the aggregate. Calendar keys and reference paths do
not preserve rule fields. E_MAINTAIN_READ_RULE names missing fields; state an
explicit `read <-` when the aggregate needs its own audience. An explicit
rule always wins. Residency is unchanged: sync is inherited only when all
source audience columns survive as bare keys, without the source horizon;
otherwise the aggregate stays server-only. `online only` opts out.

E_SYNC on a public inherited view names the source, the missing horizon and
the conflicting explicit read. An owner-only read does not make public sync
private. E_QUARRY_SYNTAX refuses a separate sync line on any view. On this public inherited view, remove that
line, then keep the public read (or omit it) for the public offline summary,
or use `online only` and lose offline aggregate/absence coverage. Never narrow
the public source to make the view private. `guide offline-interactions` is
the example and replica census; N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED links it too. For viewer-owned
existence on an eligible public window, that card shows source owner-key
coverage with ordinary `exists` instead of a summary; it does not supply totals.

E_MAINTAIN_READ_EXPIRE means an aggregate over an expiring source has a read
rule that uses fields its group keys do not determine. This includes a source
with an effective deadline inherited through `read through`. Use `read <- deny`
and replace deny with a rule over the group keys, omit `read` to inherit the
source rule, or use `online only` with an explicit key rule. Neither a source
cap nor `online only` permits a value-dependent rule. Non-expiring sources
may authorize their stored value because it is already the live value.

A predicate accepts scalar comparisons, and/or/not, boolean literals and bare

   328	/// The module, or why the case is outside the fragment.
   329	pub fn module(source: &str) -> Result<LeanModule, String> {
   330	    let (document, diagnostics) = snapback4_lang::parse_document("schema.q", source);
   331	    if !diagnostics.is_empty() {
   332	        return Err("parse".into());
   333	    }
   334	    snapback4_lang::emit::lean::from_documents(&[document]).map_err(|u| u.reason)
   335	}
   336	
   337	/// Compare one case whose source compiles. `Err` is a harness or emitter
   338	/// defect (never a verdict).
   339	pub fn compare(case: &Case, source: &str) -> Result<Checked, String> {
   340	    let unsupported = |reason: String| Ok(Checked { verdict: Verdict::Unsupported(reason), executed: 0, pages: 0, mutation: false, class: String::new() });
   341	    let module = match module(source) {
   342	        Ok(m) => m,
   343	        Err(reason) => return unsupported(reason),
   344	    };
   345	    let mutation = case.program.mutation();
   346	    if let Err(u) = module.program("run") {
   347	        return unsupported(u.reason);
   348	    }
   349	    if mutation {
   350	        if let Err(u) = module.program("observe") {
   351	            return unsupported(format!("observe-{}", u.reason));
   352	        }
   353	    }
   354	    let store = match module.store(&case.rows) {
   355	        Ok(s) => s,
   356	        Err(u) => return unsupported(u.reason),
   357	    };
   358	    let mut m_args = match module.tag_args("run", &case.args) {
   359	        Ok(a) => a,
   360	        Err(u) => return unsupported(u.reason),
   361	    };
   362	    // The prelude: each step on the engine and on the model's store, in order.
   363	    for i in 0..case.steps.len() {
   364	        if let Err(u) = module.program(&format!("prep{i}")) {
   365	            return unsupported(format!("prelude-{}", u.reason));
   366	        }
   367	        if let Err(u) = module.tag_args(&format!("prep{i}"), &case.steps[i].args) {
   368	            return unsupported(format!("prelude-{}", u.reason));
   369	        }
   370	    }

    31	pub(crate) struct GuardedRows<'a> { raw: &'a dyn Rows, remaining: Cell<usize>, authorizing: Cell<bool>, reference_prepaid: Cell<bool>, revision: Cell<u64>, lookup_cache: RefCell<Option<(String, String, Vec<Value>, Option<Value>)>> }

    33	    pub(crate) fn new(raw: &'a dyn Rows) -> Self { Self { raw, remaining: Cell::new(AUTHORIZATION_WALK_LIMIT), authorizing: Cell::new(false), reference_prepaid: Cell::new(false), revision: Cell::new(0), lookup_cache: RefCell::new(None) } }
    34	    pub(super) fn revision(&self) -> u64 { self.revision.get() }
    35	    pub(super) fn remaining(&self) -> usize { self.remaining.get() }
    36	    fn limit(&self, table: &str, requested: usize) -> Result<usize, RuntimeError> {
    37	        if !self.authorizing.get() || self.reference_prepaid.get() { return Ok(requested); }
    38	        if self.remaining.get() == 0 { return Err(RuntimeError::AuthorizationBudget { table: table.into(), effect: crate::schema::Effect::Read }); }
    39	        Ok(requested.min(self.remaining.get()))
    40	    }
    41	    fn charge(&self, fetched: usize) {
    42	        if self.authorizing.get() && !self.reference_prepaid.get() { self.remaining.set(self.remaining.get().saturating_sub(fetched)); }
    43	    }
    44	    /// Return the walk allowance of a fetched row the store stepped over as
    45	    /// expired: such rows are absent, so they cannot exhaust a read's walk.
    46	    pub(super) fn refund(&self, fetched: usize) {
    47	        if self.authorizing.get() { self.remaining.set(self.remaining.get() + fetched); }
    48	    }

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-device/tests/reference_actions.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-device/tests/reference_actions.rs
index 06934c8233..8f724f137e 100644
--- a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-device/tests/reference_actions.rs
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-device/tests/reference_actions.rs
@@ -77,6 +77,42 @@ fn task_ids<S: DeviceStore>(d: &mut Device<S>) -> Json {
 }
 
 fn run<H: Host>(host: &mut H) {
+    // Server expiry is a physical retirement with the schema's declared
+    // referential effects. Devices receive that one committed closure just as
+    // they receive an authored parent delete.
+    let expiring = r#"
+table parents:
+  until: time
+  expire at .until
+  public 'parents'
+  sync public
+table cascadeChildren:
+  parent: parents on delete cascade
+  public 'cascadeChildren'
+  sync public
+table nullableChildren:
+  parent: parents ? on delete set null
+  public 'nullableChildren'
+  sync public
+"#;
+    let (mut e, b) = compile(expiring);
+    e.seed_at([
+        ("parents".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("p")), ("until", snapback4_core::Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("cascadeChildren".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("c")), ("parent", id("p"))])),
+        ("nullableChildren".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("n")), ("parent", id("p"))])),
+    ], 0).unwrap();
+    let mut d = host.open("expiry-actions", Some(b)).unwrap();
+    acquire(&mut e, &mut d, "alice", 0);
+    let from = d.state().unwrap().0;
+    assert_eq!(e.sweep_expired(10, 100).unwrap(), 1);
+    d.apply(&stream_page(&mut e, "alice", from), false).unwrap();
+    assert!(d.store().raw_rows("parents").is_empty());
+    assert!(d.store().raw_rows("cascadeChildren").is_empty());
+    let nullable = d.store().raw_rows("nullableChildren");
+    assert_eq!(nullable.len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(nullable[0].field("parent").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Null);
+    host.close("expiry-actions", d);
+
     // Every referencing group is public and held: the cascade predicts, deleting the held tasks.
     let (mut e, b) = compile(PUBLIC);
     assert_eq!(write(&mut e, "setup", "alice", &[]), "ok");
@@ -173,6 +209,55 @@ mutation drop(id: parents):
     host.close("retained-cascade", d);
 }
 
+#[test]
+fn authoritative_memory_sweeps_resume_and_wrap_past_restricted_roots() {
+    let source = r#"
+table roots:
+  expiresAt: time ?
+  expire at .expiresAt
+  public 'roots'
+table cascadeKids:
+  root: roots on delete cascade
+  cap 4 by root
+  public 'cascade kids'
+table nullableKids:
+  root: roots ? on delete set null
+  cap 4 by root
+  public 'nullable kids'
+table blockers:
+  root: roots
+  public 'blockers'
+"#;
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    let mut store = snapback4_device::memory::MemoryStore::new();
+    store.activate_server_at(bound.schema, 0).unwrap();
+    store.seed_server_at([
+        ("roots".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("a-blocked")), ("expiresAt", snapback4_core::Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("roots".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("b-blocked")), ("expiresAt", snapback4_core::Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("roots".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("c-blocked")), ("expiresAt", snapback4_core::Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("roots".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("z-free")), ("expiresAt", snapback4_core::Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("cascadeKids".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("cz")), ("root", id("z-free"))])),
+        ("nullableKids".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("nz")), ("root", id("z-free"))])),
+        ("blockers".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("ba")), ("root", id("a-blocked"))])),
+        ("blockers".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("bb")), ("root", id("b-blocked"))])),
+        ("blockers".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("bc")), ("root", id("c-blocked"))])),
+    ], 0).unwrap();
+    let wrapped_removed: u64 = (0..4).map(|_| store.sweep_server_at(10, 1).unwrap()).sum();
+    assert_eq!(wrapped_removed, 1, "continuation reaches the free root within one bounded turn per due root");
+    assert!(store.raw_rows("cascadeKids").is_empty());
+    assert_eq!(store.raw_rows("nullableKids")[0].field("root").unwrap(), &snapback4_core::Value::Null);
+
+    // The cursor is after z. A lower new key is found after the scan wraps;
+    // each restricted root still consumes one bounded visit.
+    store.seed_server_at([("roots".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
+        ("id", id("aa-free")), ("expiresAt", snapback4_core::Value::Int(10)),
+    ]))], 0).unwrap();
+    let wrapped_removed: u64 = (0..4).map(|_| store.sweep_server_at(10, 1).unwrap()).sum();
+    assert_eq!(wrapped_removed, 1, "a wrapped scan reaches the new lower key within one bounded turn per due root");
+    assert!(!store.raw_rows("roots").iter().any(|row| row.field("id").unwrap() == &id("aa-free")));
+}
+
 #[test] fn reference_actions_memory() { run(&mut Memory::new()); }
 #[test] fn reference_actions_sqlite() { run(&mut Sqlite::new("reference-actions")); }
 
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4-sim/tests/expiry_reference_actions.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-sim/tests/expiry_reference_actions.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..05a91f6ddf
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4-sim/tests/expiry_reference_actions.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
+//! Expiry is a physical delete trigger for referential actions (LLP 3000.039
+//! §3). These cases cover scheduled cleanup, commit-end retirement and
+//! same-id replacement of an expired row against the real engine.
+use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value, WriteState, value::object};
+use std::collections::BTreeMap;
+
+fn id(value: &str) -> Value { Value::Id(value.into()) }
+
+fn engine(source: &str) -> Engine {
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap()
+}
+
+const SWEEP: &str = r#"
+table roots:
+  expiresAt: time ?
+  by byExpiry: expiresAt
+  expire at .expiresAt
+  public 'roots'
+  sync public
+table cascadeKids:
+  root: roots on delete cascade
+  cap 4 by root
+  public 'cascade kids'
+  sync public
+table nullableKids:
+  root: roots ? on delete set null
+  cap 4 by root
+  public 'nullable kids'
+  sync public
+table blockers:
+  root: roots
+  public 'blockers'
+  sync public
+"#;
+
+#[test]
+fn sweep_applies_every_action_and_advances_past_a_restricted_root() {
+    let mut e = engine(SWEEP);
+    e.seed_at([
+        ("roots".into(), object([("id", id("a-blocked")), ("expiresAt", Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("roots".into(), object([("id", id("b-blocked")), ("expiresAt", Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("roots".into(), object([("id", id("c-blocked")), ("expiresAt", Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("roots".into(), object([("id", id("z-free")), ("expiresAt", Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("cascadeKids".into(), object([("id", id("ca")), ("root", id("a-blocked"))])),
+        ("cascadeKids".into(), object([("id", id("cz")), ("root", id("z-free"))])),
+        ("nullableKids".into(), object([("id", id("nz")), ("root", id("z-free"))])),
+        ("blockers".into(), object([("id", id("ba")), ("root", id("a-blocked"))])),
+        ("blockers".into(), object([("id", id("bb")), ("root", id("b-blocked"))])),
+        ("blockers".into(), object([("id", id("bc")), ("root", id("c-blocked"))])),
+    ], 0).unwrap();
+
+    for expected in [0, 0, 0, 1] {
+        assert_eq!(e.sweep_expired(10, 1).unwrap(), expected, "a one-row sweep must advance its physical scan position");
+    }
+    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("roots").unwrap().iter().map(|r| r.field("id").unwrap().as_str().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["a-blocked", "b-blocked", "c-blocked"]);
+    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("cascadeKids").unwrap().iter().map(|r| r.field("id").unwrap().as_str().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["ca"]);
+    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("nullableKids").unwrap()[0].field("root").unwrap(), &Value::Null);
+
+    for blocker in ["ba", "bb", "bc"] { e.seed_delete("blockers", blocker).unwrap(); }
+    assert_eq!(e.sweep_expired(10, 10).unwrap(), 3);
+    assert!(e.store().rows("roots").unwrap().is_empty());
+    assert!(e.store().rows("cascadeKids").unwrap().is_empty());
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn a_late_cascade_refusal_rolls_back_earlier_set_null_writes() {
+    let source = r#"
+table roots:
+  expiresAt: time ?
+  by byExpiry: expiresAt
+  expire at .expiresAt
+  public 'roots'
+table nullableKids:
+  root: roots ? on delete set null
+  cap 1 by root
+  public 'nullable kids'
+table children:
+  root: roots on delete cascade
+  cap 1 by root
+  public 'children'
+table grandchildren:
+  child: children
+  read through .child
+"#;
+    let mut e = engine(source);
+    e.seed_at([
+        ("roots".into(), object([("id", id("p")), ("expiresAt", Value::Int(10))])),
+        ("nullableKids".into(), object([("id", id("n")), ("root", id("p"))])),
+        ("children".into(), object([("id", id("c")), ("root", id("p"))])),
+        ("grandchildren".into(), object([("id", id("g")), ("child", id("c"))])),
+    ], 0).unwrap();
+
+    let error = e.sweep_expired(10, 10).unwrap_err();
+    assert_eq!(error.code(), "E_PARENT_HAS_CHILDREN", "{error:?}");
+    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("nullableKids").unwrap()[0].field("root").unwrap(), &id("p"), "the prior set-null write rolled back");
+    for table in ["roots", "children", "grandchildren"] {
+        assert_eq!(e.store().rows(table).unwrap().len(), 1, "{table}");
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn commit_retirement_and_expired_same_id_replacement_apply_actions() {
+    let source = format!(r#"{SWEEP}
+mutation bornExpired():
+  insert roots {{id: 'born', expiresAt: now}}
+  insert cascadeKids {{id: 'born-c', root: 'born'}}
+  insert nullableKids {{id: 'born-n', root: 'born'}}
+  return null
+mutation bornRestricted():
+  insert roots {{id: 'blocked', expiresAt: now}}
+  insert blockers {{id: 'blocked-b', root: 'blocked'}}
+  return null
+mutation replaceExpired():
+  insert roots {{id: 'old', expiresAt: null}}
+  return null
+"#);
+    let mut e = engine(&source);
+    let sent = e.mutate("bornExpired", None, Context::new("dev:alice", BTreeMap::new()).at(100)).unwrap();
+    assert!(matches!(sent.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{:?}", sent.state);
+    assert!(e.store().rows("roots").unwrap().is_empty());
+    assert!(e.store().rows("cascadeKids").unwrap().is_empty());
+    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("nullableKids").unwrap()[0].field("root").unwrap(), &Value::Null);
+
+    let refused = e.mutate("bornRestricted", None, Context::new("dev:alice", BTreeMap::new()).at(100)).unwrap();
+    assert!(matches!(refused.state, WriteState::Failed { ref why, .. } if why.code == "E_REFERENCED"), "{:?}", refused.state);
+    assert!(e.store().rows("roots").unwrap().is_empty(), "the refused birth and its blocker roll back together");
+    assert!(e.store().rows("blockers").unwrap().is_empty());
+
+    e.seed_at([
+        ("roots".into(), object([("id", id("old")), ("expiresAt", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("cascadeKids".into(), object([("id", id("old-c")), ("root", id("old"))])),
+    ], 0).unwrap();
+    let replaced = e.mutate("replaceExpired", None, Context::new("dev:alice", BTreeMap::new()).at(100)).unwrap();
+    assert!(matches!(replaced.state, WriteState::Sent { .. }), "{:?}", replaced.state);
+    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("roots").unwrap().len(), 1);
+    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("roots").unwrap()[0].field("expiresAt").unwrap(), &Value::Null);
+    assert!(e.store().rows("cascadeKids").unwrap().is_empty(), "same-id cleanup cannot orphan the expired predecessor's child");
+}
diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/aggregate_identity_upgrade.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/aggregate_identity_upgrade.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..c6754a981f
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/aggregate_identity_upgrade.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,175 @@
+//! A store written by the frozen 0.4.28 binary adopts digest-addressed
+//! aggregate rows before the current engine serves it.
+use rusqlite::Connection;
+use std::{
+    fs,
+    path::{Path, PathBuf},
+    process::Command,
+};
+
+const SOURCE: &str = r#"use personas alice
+table entries:
+  group: text <=256
+  amount: int
+  insert <- allow
+  public
+view totals: sum entries of .amount by .group
+  public
+mutation add(group: text <=256, amount: int):
+  insert entries {group, amount}
+query total(group: text <=256):
+  return totals[group]
+"#;
+
+struct Temp(PathBuf);
+impl Temp {
+    fn new() -> Self {
+        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!(
+            "snapback4-maintained-id-{}-{}",
+            std::process::id(),
+            std::time::SystemTime::now()
+                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
+                .unwrap()
+                .as_nanos()
+        ));
+        fs::create_dir_all(path.join("snapback")).unwrap();
+        fs::write(path.join("snapback/schema.q"), SOURCE).unwrap();
+        Self(path)
+    }
+}
+impl Drop for Temp {
+    fn drop(&mut self) {
+        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
+    }
+}
+
+fn aggregate_id(store: &Path) -> String {
+    Connection::open(store)
+        .unwrap()
+        .query_row(
+            "SELECT r.id FROM r JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=r.t WHERE c.name='totals'",
+            [],
+            |row| row.get(0),
+        )
+        .unwrap()
+}
+
+#[test]
+#[ignore = "requires frozen 0.4.28 binary via SNAPBACK4_BASELINE_BIN"]
+fn a_0_4_28_store_rebuilds_legacy_aggregate_ids_and_fences_old_devices() {
+    let baseline = PathBuf::from(
+        std::env::var_os("SNAPBACK4_BASELINE_BIN")
+            .expect("set SNAPBACK4_BASELINE_BIN to the frozen 0.4.28 snapback4 binary"),
+    );
+    assert!(
+        baseline.is_file(),
+        "SNAPBACK4_BASELINE_BIN is not a file: {}",
+        baseline.display()
+    );
+    let version = Command::new(&baseline).arg("--version").output().unwrap();
+    assert!(version.status.success());
+    assert!(
+        String::from_utf8_lossy(&version.stdout).contains("0.4.28"),
+        "unexpected baseline: {:?}",
+        version
+    );
+
+    let dir = Temp::new();
+    let group = "x".repeat(200);
+    let args = serde_json::json!({"group": group, "amount": 7}).to_string();
+    let written = Command::new(&baseline)
+        .current_dir(&dir.0)
+        .args(["run", "add", &args, "--as", "alice", "--now", "1", "--json"])
+        .output()
+        .unwrap();
+    assert!(
+        written.status.success(),
+        "{}",
+        String::from_utf8_lossy(&written.stderr)
+    );
+    let store = dir.0.join(".snapback4/store.sqlite");
+    let legacy = aggregate_id(&store);
+    assert!(
+        legacy.len() > snapback4_core::rows::MAX_ID_BYTES,
+        "the frozen binary must have written the oversized legacy address"
+    );
+    let before: u64 = Connection::open(&store)
+        .unwrap()
+        .query_row(
+            "SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='generation'",
+            [],
+            |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
+        )
+        .unwrap()
+        .parse()
+        .unwrap();
+
+    let opened = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4"))
+        .current_dir(&dir.0)
+        .args([
+            "run",
+            "total",
+            &serde_json::json!({"group": group}).to_string(),
+            "--as",
+            "alice",
+            "--now",
+            "2",
+            "--json",
+        ])
+        .output()
+        .unwrap();
+    assert!(
+        opened.status.success(),
+        "stdout={} stderr={}",
+        String::from_utf8_lossy(&opened.stdout),
+        String::from_utf8_lossy(&opened.stderr)
+    );
+    let answer: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&opened.stdout).unwrap();
+    assert_eq!(answer["data"]["value"], 7, "{answer}");
+
+    let digest = aggregate_id(&store);
+    assert_eq!(digest.len(), 64);
+    assert_ne!(digest, legacy);
+    let conn = Connection::open(&store).unwrap();
+    let (after, marker): (u64, String) = (
+        conn.query_row(
+            "SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='generation'",
+            [],
+            |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
+        )
+        .unwrap()
+        .parse()
+        .unwrap(),
+        conn.query_row(
+            "SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key=?1",
+            [snapback4_core::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META],
+            |row| row.get(0),
+        )
+        .unwrap(),
+    );
+    assert!(
+        after > before,
+        "identity adoption must publish a new device generation"
+    );
+    assert_eq!(marker, snapback4_core::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
+
+    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), SOURCE.into())]);
+    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
+    let required =
+        snapback4_core::device_protocol::compatibility(&bound.schema.public_projection());
+    assert!(
+        required
+            .capabilities
+            .contains(&"maintained-id-sha256-v1".to_owned())
+    );
+    let mut old = snapback4_core::device_protocol::DeviceEngine::current();
+    old.version = "0.4.28".into();
+    old.capabilities
+        .retain(|capability| capability != "maintained-id-sha256-v1");
+    let refusal = snapback4_core::device_protocol::check(Some(&old), &required).unwrap_err();
+    assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_DEVICE_VERSION");
+    assert!(
+        refusal.message.contains("maintained-id-sha256-v1"),
+        "{refusal:?}"
+    );
+}

   135	    fn has_receipt_columns(&self) -> bool {

   137	            self.receipt_dependencies.get(name).is_some_and(|fields| fields.contains(field))))
   138	    }
   139	    pub fn required_capabilities(&self) -> BTreeSet<String> {
   140	        CAPABILITIES.iter().filter(|(_, needs)| needs(self)).map(|(name, _)| (*name).into()).collect()
   141	    }
   142	}
   143	
   144	impl Serialize for Schema {
   145	    fn serialize<S: Serializer>(&self, serializer: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
   146	        let required = self.required_capabilities();
   147	        // The derive's field order, omissions and bytes are unchanged for
   148	        // ordinary schemas, including the serialized conformance corpus.
   149	        if required.is_empty() { return Wire::serialize(self, serializer); }
   150	        let mut wire = self.clone();
   151	        for table in wire.tables.values_mut() {
   152	            table.constraints.retain(|c| !matches!(c, Constraint::Recorded));
   153	        }
   154	        let mut carrier = wire.tables.first_entry().ok_or_else(||
   155	            serde::ser::Error::custom("E_SCHEMA_CAPABILITY: required capabilities need a table carrier"))?;
   156	        carrier.get_mut().constraints.push(Constraint::Capabilities(required));
   157	        Wire::serialize(&wire, serializer)
   158	    }
   159	}
   160	
   161	impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for Schema {
   162	    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
   163	        // The private remote derive uses the ordinary
   164	        // streaming serde codec. No JSON-value or content-buffer replay.
   165	        let mut schema = Wire::deserialize(deserializer)?;
   166	        let mut declared = BTreeSet::new();
   167	        let mut carriers = 0;
   168	        let mut legacy = false;
   169	        for table in schema.tables.values_mut() {
   170	            let mut recorded = 0;
   171	            table.constraints.retain(|constraint| {
   172	                if let Constraint::Capabilities(names) = constraint {
   173	                    carriers += 1;
   174	                    declared.extend(names.iter().cloned());
   175	                    false
   176	                } else if matches!(constraint, Constraint::Recorded) {
   177	                    recorded += 1;
   178	                    false
   179	                } else { true }
   180	            });
   181	            if recorded > 1 || (recorded != 0 && table.recorded.is_empty()) {

    43	    // only the server reads private totals or performs their mirrored writes.

    45	    pub(super) fn birth_columns(&mut self, name: &str, mut row: Value) -> Result<Value,RuntimeError> {
    46	        let price = self.source_price(name, crate::schema::reservations::BIRTH)?;
    47	        self.reservation_deferred |= self.device && price[2] != 0;

    53	        if declarations.is_empty() { return Ok(row); }

    62	    }

    30	impl Runner<'_> {
    31	    /// An authoritative in-memory generation rebuilds before publishing its
    32	    /// staged store. Ordinary device installation does not call this path.
    33	    pub fn rebuild_server_derivations(&mut self, previous: Option<&Schema>) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    34	        let saved=self.event_free;self.event_free=true;
    35	        let result=(|| {
    36	            for m in self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.is_none()){self.rebuild_maintain(m)?;}
    37	            self.activate_columns(previous,&self.schema.maintains.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),&changed_parents(self.schema,previous), None)
    38	        })();
    39	        self.event_free=saved;result
    40	    }
    41	    pub(super) fn activate_columns(&mut self, previous: Option<&Schema>, rebuild: &[&Maintain], parents: &BTreeSet<String>, mut report: Option<&mut crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    42	        let saved = self.event_free;
    43	        self.event_free = true;
    44	        let result = (|| {
    45	            // Even an all-zero addition/removal wakes server-run parent watches.
    46	            self.written_tables.extend(parents.iter().cloned());
    47	            for m in rebuild.iter().filter(|m| m.private.is_some()) {
    48	                if let Some(report) = report.as_deref_mut() { self.rebuild_maintain_reported(m, report)?; } else { self.rebuild_maintain(m)?; }
    49	            }
    50	            for m in self.schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.private.is_some()) {
    51	                let owner = m.private.as_ref().unwrap();
    52	                if rebuild.contains(&m) || previous.is_some_and(|old| lifetime_changed(self.schema, old, &owner.table)) {
    53	                    self.mirror_column_pass(m)?;
    54	                }
    55	            }
    56	            Ok(())
    57	        })();
    58	        self.event_free = saved;
    59	        result
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    pub(super) fn mirror_column_pass(&mut self, m: &Maintain) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    63	        let owner = m.private.as_ref().expect("private maintain");
    64	        let mut bounds = Bounds::default();
    65	        loop {
    66	            let page = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.scan(allowance, &owner.table, "byId", &[], &bounds, Dir::Asc, 256))?;
    67	            if page.is_empty() { break; }
    68	            for (row, key) in page {
    69	                bounds.after = crate::key::decode(&key);
    70	                let group = vec![row.field("id")?.clone()];
    71	                let total = self.metered_scan(|runner, allowance| runner.tx.get(allowance, &m.target, &maintained_id(&group)))?
    72	                    .and_then(|r| r.field("value").ok().cloned()).unwrap_or(Value::Int(0)).as_int()?;
    73	                // Re-read the current parent in mirror_column: several columns
    74	                // must compose. Missing zero stays physically absent.
    75	                self.mirror_column(m, &group, total)?;
    76	            }
    77	        }
    78	        Ok(())
    79	    }
    80	}

use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{engine::LoadMode, schema::{ColumnDefault, ColumnType}, value::object};

#[test]
fn expired_unswept_parent_is_never_physically_written_and_lifetime_edits_reconcile() {
    let source = MODEL.replacen("  at: time", "  until: time ?\n  by byUntil: until, id\n  expire at .until\n  at: time", 1);
    let mut b = compile(&source);
    let parent = b.schema.tables.get_mut("posts").unwrap();
    parent.columns.insert("until".into(), ColumnType::Time);
    parent.defaults.insert("until".into(), ColumnDefault::Literal(Value::Int(900)));
    let mut e = Engine::memory(b.schema.clone(), b.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    e.load_mode([("posts".into(), object([("id", id("p")), ("at", Value::Int(1)), ("until", Value::Int(900))]))], LoadMode::Restore).unwrap();
    // A legacy image missing a required literal-default field is interpreted
    // through that default, including in Table::expired.
    e.store().conn().execute_batch("UPDATE r SET data = json_remove(data, '$.Row.until') WHERE t = (SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name = 'posts');").unwrap();
    let before = e.store().rows("posts").unwrap();
    assert!(before[0].field("until").is_err());
    assert!(e.schema().tables["posts"].expired(&before[0],1000));
    let check = |e: &Engine, count: i64, total: i64| {
        assert_eq!(e.store().rows("likes").unwrap().len(),count as usize);
        for m in &e.schema().maintains {
            let rows=e.store().rows(&m.target).unwrap();
            if count==0 {assert!(rows.is_empty());} else {
                assert_eq!(rows[0].field("value").unwrap(),&Value::Int(if m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column=="total" {total} else {count}));
            }
        }
    };
    e.store().conn().execute_batch("CREATE TRIGGER no_expired_mirror BEFORE UPDATE ON r WHEN NEW.t = (SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name = 'posts') BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'expired parent written'); END;").unwrap();
    like(&mut e, "l", id("p"), true, 3);
    check(&e,1,3);
    mutate(&mut e, "unlike", &[("id", id("l"))]);
    check(&e,0,0);
    let mut now_unspecified = context(&[("id", id("clock")), ("postId", id("p")), ("active", Value::Bool(true)), ("amount", Value::Int(1))]);
    now_unspecified.now = None;
    assert!(matches!(e.mutate("like", None, now_unspecified).unwrap().state, WriteState::Sent { .. }));
    check(&e,1,1);
    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("posts").unwrap(), before);
    // Reviving the defaulted missing expiry must mirror the current total.
    e.store().conn().execute_batch("DROP TRIGGER no_expired_mirror;").unwrap();
    let mut candidate = b.schema.clone();
    candidate.tables.get_mut("posts").unwrap().columns.insert("until".into(), ColumnType::Optional(Box::new(ColumnType::Time)));
    e.adopt(candidate, b.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(totals(&mut e,"p"),(1,1,1));

}

#[test]
fn resolved_source_edits_rebuild_with_only_source_rows_populated() {
    for edit in ["kind", "predicate", "optional", "default"] {
        let mut e = engine(MODEL);
        like(&mut e, "l", id("missing"), true, 3);
        assert!(e.store().rows("posts").unwrap().is_empty());
        let source = match edit {
            "kind" => MODEL.replace("likeCount: count likes by postId", "likeCount: sum likes of amount by postId"),
            "predicate" => MODEL.replace("likeCount: count likes by postId", "likeCount: count likes where .active by postId"),
            "optional" => MODEL.to_owned(),
            _ => MODEL.replace("  active: bool", "  active: bool default true"),
        };
        let mut b = compile(&source);
        if edit == "optional" {
            let kind = ColumnType::Ref { table: "posts".into() };
            b.schema.tables.get_mut("likes").unwrap().columns.insert("postId".into(), kind.clone());
            for maintain in &b.schema.maintains { b.schema.tables.get_mut(&maintain.target).unwrap().columns.insert(snapback4_core::schema::columns::GROUP.into(), kind.clone()); }
        }
        e.adopt(b.schema, b.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
        post(&mut e, "missing", 1);
        assert_eq!(totals(&mut e, "missing"), (if edit == "kind" {3} else {1}, 1, 3));
    }
}

#[test]
fn restore_preserves_values_import_rebuilds_and_neither_publishes_private_names() {
    let mut e = engine(&MODEL.replace("  online only", "  sync public"));
    like(&mut e, "l", id("p"), true, 3);
    let row = object([("id", id("p")), ("at", Value::Int(1)), ("likeCount", Value::Int(77)), ("selected", Value::Int(78)), ("total", Value::Int(79))]);
    let private: Vec<_> = e.schema().maintains.iter().map(|m| {
        let value = row.field(&m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column).unwrap().clone();
        (m.target.clone(), object([("id", id(&snapback4_core::schema::maintained_id(&[id("p")]))),
            (snapback4_core::schema::columns::GROUP, id("p")), ("value", value)]))
    }).collect();
    let from = e.seq().unwrap();
    e.load_mode(std::iter::once(("posts".into(), row.clone())).chain(private.clone()), LoadMode::Restore).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.store().rows("posts").unwrap(), vec![row]);
    for (target, image) in &private { assert_eq!(e.store().rows(target).unwrap(), vec![image.clone()]); }
    assert_eq!(totals(&mut e, "p"), (77, 78, 79));
    assert!(!serde_json::to_string(&e.stream("alice", from, 100).unwrap()).unwrap().contains("$column:"));
    let metadata: Vec<String> = e.store().conn().prepare("SELECT touched FROM _commits").unwrap().query_map([], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap().collect::<Result<_, _>>().unwrap();
    assert!(metadata.iter().all(|s| !s.contains("$column:")));
    let before = e.seq().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(e.load(private).unwrap_err().code(), "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE");
    assert_eq!(e.seq().unwrap(), before);
    e.load([("posts".into(), object([("id", id("p")), ("at", Value::Int(1)), ("likeCount", Value::Int(999))]))]).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(totals(&mut e, "p"), (1, 1, 3));
    for m in &e.schema().maintains {
        let expected = if m.private.as_ref().unwrap().column == "total" { 3 } else { 1 };
        assert_eq!(e.store().rows(&m.target).unwrap()[0].field("value").unwrap(), &Value::Int(expected));
    }
    let metadata: Vec<String> = e.store().conn().prepare("SELECT touched FROM _commits").unwrap().query_map([], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap().collect::<Result<_, _>>().unwrap();
    assert!(metadata.iter().all(|s| !s.contains("$column:")));
    assert!(!serde_json::to_string(&e.stream("alice", before, 100).unwrap()).unwrap().contains("$column:"));
}

    for op in ["post", "absent", "put"] {
        let mut e = engine(&source);
        like(&mut e, "l", id("p"), true, 7);
        let args = if op == "post" { vec![("id", id("p")), ("at", Value::Int(1))] } else { vec![("id", id("p"))] };
